# Execution Tracker — Master Plan 006

**Created**: 2026-04-13
**Source of truth**: [specs/006-master-plan/MASTER_PLAN.md](../../specs/006-master-plan/MASTER_PLAN.md) + [ADR-008](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-008-api-first-pivot.md)
**Update cadence**: weekly (every Monday), or on phase-boundary events

---

## How to use this file

- Every top-level task has an owner and a state: `todo` / `doing` / `blocked` / `done` / `dropped`.
- Blockers are explicit; if a task is blocked, the blocker row must name what's blocking and who owns unblocking it.
- External dependencies (Etsy scope review, Gearment sandbox creds) have their own section — these are **outside** the team's control and need separate status pings.
- Phases follow MASTER_PLAN §4. A task can only move to `doing` if the phase it belongs to has started.
- Do not edit task IDs; append new tasks at the bottom of the relevant phase.

### 🎯 Active prioritization — coding-first to reach end-to-end pipeline test (set 2026-04-27)

**Owner directive (2026-04-27)**: Finish the coding chain that lets us run an order **end-to-end** (ingest → fulfill → track) on staging before touching reporting/observability surfaces. Reporting is Phase-1 polish, not a Phase-0 blocker.

**Critical path for E2E coding** (do these next, in order):

1. **P0-20** — module decomposition into 4 modules (architectural prerequisite for all Phase 1 code)
2. **P1-05** — `sale.order.fulfillment` delegation mixin (must land before any dashboard write hits `sale.order`)
3. **P1-06** — unified `shipping.carrier` model + seed (consumed by both Etsy push + GKE import)
4. **P0-14..17** — Spec 005 sandbox (OAuth PKCE → ApiClient → OrderSyncer → api.log) — code against dev token while E1 bakes
4b. **P0-22** — Etsy ingest parity (API ↔ email-parser produce identical `sale.order` / `sale.order.line`) — gates any production cutover from email to API _(added 2026-05-10)_
5. **P0-18** — Gearment spike (unblocked partial 2026-04-27); start dashboard-driven discovery to unblock P4-01
6. **P1-04** — address-change approval workflow (safety-critical; small surface)
7. **P1-01** — Order Dashboard (the operator entry point)
7b. **P1-LBL** — `label_status` Selection → `label.status.option` Many2one + JPG seed (prerequisite for P1-01b) _(added 2026-05-10)_
7c. **P1-01b** — Order-line dashboard refactor (model swap `sale.order` → `sale.order.line`, lift Excel column layout, keep BA/Marketing/PD columns) _(added 2026-05-10)_
8. **P2-01..05** — tracking import wizard + carrier auto-detect (closes the loop back to Etsy push)
9. **P4-01** — Gearment adapter (outbound fulfillment) — needs P0-18 + E2 API keys; **2026-05-10 scope expansion**: form button (whole-order push) + Ops Dashboard per-line/bulk-line sync action + tracking writeback surfaced read-only in Etsy tab

**Operational cutover gates** (post-coding, before P1-11 pilot flip — added 2026-05-21):

- **P1-11-DEPLOY-STAGING** — *done 2026-05-21*. Staging refreshed: `etsy_integration` → 19.0.2.7.0 (post-P1-XAPI), `mhc` 19.0.1.0.36, `mhf` 19.0.1.0.21 all installed; `etsy_shop` schema migrated (7→19 cols, `active_source` mapped); secrets bind-mount + credentials file + `etsy.oauth.credentials_path` system param all in place. Runbook §5.1 prereqs are now reachable on staging; jahandmadeart pilot flip (P1-11) is unblocked.
- **P1-XAPI-SHARED-SECRET-HEADER** — *done 2026-05-21*. Etsy's 2026-02-09 enforcement requires `x-api-key: keystring:shared_secret`; our `EtsyApiClient._session()` was still emitting keystring-only. Fixed before P1-11 dispatch — otherwise the OAuth callback would succeed but every subsequent API call would 401. Also retracted the previous "10-char secret looks short" caveat: Etsy's own Quickstart example uses a 10-char shared_secret (`a1b2c3d4e5`); the owner-supplied value is fine.
- **E2 Gearment sandbox API keys** (owner) — required for the tracking-back leg of the runbook §5.3 round-trip verify. Without it, the pilot test ends at order ingest.

**Deferred until E2E green** (reporting/hardening — pick up in Phase 1 polish):

- ~~P0-11 `multichannel.sync.health` dashboard tile~~ (model already shipped under W3.1; tile can wait)
- ~~P0-12 ban `_logger.info`~~ (cleanup, not feature)
- ~~P0-13 indexes~~ (perf, only matters once we have prod-scale traffic)
- ~~P0-19 GKE schema fingerprint~~ (defensive; fold into P2-01)
- ~~P4-03 Spec 006 Sales Pricing Audit~~ (pure reporting; explicitly Phase 4)

**Phase 0 exit criteria revised**: drop "Health dashboard green for 7 days" from must-have. Keep "BA reconciliation sign-off" + "Spec 005 client passes integration tests against dev shop with zero writes to non-dev shops" + "staging operational." Health tile becomes a Phase 1 deliverable.

Scheme cracked from Gearment OpenAPI yaml + verified against captured probe row 3 — secret is the SAME `GEARMENT_API_SECRET` value we use for outbound API auth (Gearment dashboard labels it "API signature"); `GEARMENT_WEBHOOK_HMAC_SECRET` in `.env:24` is redundant. Signing string: `url_path + nonce + timestamp + base64url(body)` (no separators, base64-URLsafe with `=` padding). Helper `_verify_signature(headers, body, env, request_path=None, now_provider=None)` exposes `request_path` so a future `/v2` route cannot replay `/v1` signatures (security review M1). 4 new fields on `gearment.api.log` (`nonce_value` indexed, `request_timestamp` Integer indexed, `signature_verified` Boolean, `verify_failure_reason` Char) + composite `(nonce_value, request_timestamp)` index. `_detect_topic` now reads body['type'] first (real Gearment uses `type`, not `event`/`topic`). On verify failure: 401 `{"error":"unauthorized"}` + audit row with truncated body (256 chars). Test vectors are SYNTHETIC (`TEST_KEY_DO_NOT_USE` + `TEST_SECRET_DO_NOT_USE_IN_PROD_64chars_padded_*`) so real `.env` Gearment credentials never land in source — captured signature recomputed against synthetic secret to keep regression vector internally consistent (security review BLOCKER fixed). 25 new tests (13 unit + 12 HttpCase) + 5 P0-18b2a tests relaxed for new contract (`assertIn(status, (200, 401))`). 132 mhf tests + 537 cross-module green. UNIQUE PARTIAL index `(nonce_value, request_timestamp) WHERE signature_verified=TRUE AND nonce_value<>'' AND request_timestamp>0` — narrowed to verified rows so failure rows can repeat (allows multiple rejected probes; only verified writes need uniqueness). Index uses DROP+CREATE because `IF NOT EXISTS` doesn't check predicate equality. Controller catches `psycopg2.IntegrityError` → 401 + cursor rollback. **Bypass policy**: fulfillment writes use `with_context(bypass_address_change_check=True)` because webhook is system-trusted inbound and FR-017's interactive-operator guard is meant for human RPC races, not upstream tracking arrivals (documented in dispatcher module docstring + per security review). All ORM via `sudo()` (auth='public', no env.user with write perms). `markupsafe.escape()` wraps user-controlled strings before chatter posts. | P1-11-WIRE-LIVE | **Pre-cutover hotfix chain — surfaced during jahandmadeart pilot E2E on staging 2026-05-22.** Five interdependent defects blocked the OAuth → API → ingest path: (1) C-P1-10 scope validation hard-failed every callback because Etsy v3 token-exchange responses **do not include `scope`** (response keys: `access_token/api_key/expires_in/refresh_token/token_type/user_id` — diagnostic captured + reverted; check now gated on non-empty `scope` so the safety net stays armed if Etsy ever changes); (2) `etsy_shop._probe_api` had a constructor-signature bug (`EtsyApiClient(self.env, self)` vs `(shop)`) AND a double URL prefix (`/v3/application/openapi-ping` against `ETSY_API_BASE_URL` which already includes `/v3/application`); bare-except + `_logger.debug` swallowed the TypeError invisibly at `log_level=info`; (3) `services.etsy_api_client._read_credentials()` hardcoded `/opt/odoo/secrets/credentials.json` while staging had `etsy.oauth.credentials_path=/opt/odoo/secrets/etsy_credentials.json` — unified to honor the system param so OAuth controller and API client share one source of truth; (4) 403 handler discarded vendor body — added `_logger.warning` + body in raised `ValueError` per memory `feedback_capture_response_body_before_blackbox_probe.md`; (5) sandbox-era convention "`etsy.shop.id == Etsy API shop_id`" (memory item #132) breaks on real shops — JaHandmadeArt's Odoo PK is 10 but real Etsy shop_id is `60752333`; Etsy returned 403 `{"error":"User does not own Shop 10"}`. **Fix scope**: new `etsy.shop.etsy_api_shop_id` Char field (system-group, indexed, optional — required by convention before `active_source='api'`); plumbed through 3 caller sites (`etsy_order_syncer.sync_shop_orders`, `etsy_listing._sync_shop_listings`, `etsy_tracking_pusher.push`); 4 audit-log strings now reflect real URL; form view exposes the field. Memory follow-ups captured: (a) Etsy v3 token-exchange response has no `scope` field; (b) sandbox convention "Odoo PK == Etsy shop_id" is dead — `etsy_api_shop_id` is the canonical mapping. ### P1 Hybrid dropship + MTO re-architect (Owner directive 2026-05-03)

### P1/P2 Listings & Inventory sync (Wave 2 — unblocked by E1 approval 2026-05-12)

Net-new capability layered on top of the closed orders↔tracking cycle. Required for full-E2E sync per owner directive 2026-05-12. Endpoint inventory: `GET /v3/application/shops/{shop_id}/listings`, `GET /v3/application/listings/{listing_id}/inventory`, `PUT /v3/application/listings/{listing_id}/inventory`. Etsy inventory PUT requires **entire variant array to be re-submitted** — call `GET` first, mutate, `PUT` back.

## Phase 3 — Central product hub + Odoo→Etsy outbound publish (added 2026-05-23 per ADR-014)

**Charter**: Reverse the inbound-only posture established by ADR-008. Make Odoo the system of record for the multichannel catalog. Excel stays canonical for now (owner directive 2026-05-23 — recurring sync, not one-time migration). First write-out channel = Etsy; Amazon + ecommerce remain Phase 5. - A product flows Excel → Odoo (recurring sync) → Etsy (operator wizard publish), end-to-end on JaHandmadeArt pilot.
- Existing JaHandmadeArt listings backfilled into `product.template` non-destructively.
- SKU drift review surface operational; ≥ 1 product successfully canonicalised end-to-end (Accept canonical → auto-push to live Etsy → verified on Etsy).

| P4-01 | Spec 004b Gearment adapter — full state machine (live `confirm()`) + `sale.order` form button (whole-order push) + Ops Dashboard per-line/bulk-line sync action + tracking writeback surfaced read-only in Etsy tab | Dev B | `done` (B + C + D all landed 2026-05-10) | Phase 2 exit ✓, P0-18 ✓, P1-01b, P0-22 | **Scope expansion 2026-05-10 (owner D3+D4+D5)**: (a) Form button **"Sync to Gearment"** on `sale.order` form, **whole-order granularity only** (no per-line checkboxes on form view per owner D5), gated by `group_ba_shipping`; calls `gearment_adapter.confirm()` and writes returned `partner_ref` / tracking back through `sale.order.fulfillment`. (b) Server action `action_gearment_bulk_sync` on `operations_dashboard_list_view` (line-based per P1-01b) — operator selects N `sale.order.line` records (any orders, **any** label_status), clicks action, payload built per line, push synchronous with `bus.notification` progress notification. **No default label filter, no auto-trigger** — operator-driven only per owner D3 ("we'll back on this later"; ICP/per-shop config explicitly out of scope). (c) Etsy tab on `sale.view_order_form` (`etsy_integration/views/sale_order_views.xml:42-70`) gains a **read-only Shipping section** showing existing `sale.order.fulfillment` fields: `tracking_number`, `shipping_carrier_id`, `tracking_state`, `shipping_date`, `tracking_url` — **no new fields**, no Etsy-outbound API push (Etsy app scope review **not** needed). Per owner D4: smallest blast radius, just surface fulfillment data inside the existing Etsy tab. **Security**: form button + bulk action both gated by `group_ba_shipping` (write) + FR-017 `_check_ba_shipping_or_raise()` write-defense pattern. **Idempotency**: re-sync of an already-pushed line is a no-op (existing `gearment_payload.idempotency_key` covers this). **Ordering**: P4-01 dispatch waits for **P1-01b** (dashboard model swap to line) so the bulk action targets the correct model from the start, and **P0-22** (ingest parity) so the Gearment payload sees identical fields regardless of which ingest path produced the line. — — — **2026-05-10 readiness probe** against live `apiv2.gearment.com` surfaced 3 CRITICAL contract gaps that block dispatch as a pure state-machine slice (see `specs/004-fulfillment-routing/findings.md` §"P4-01 readiness probe"). **G1**: current `gearment_adapter.push_order` calls `POST /api/v3/orders` → live API returns **404**; canonical path is `POST /api/v3/orders/draft`. **G2**: current `gearment_payload.py` schema is wrong — real fields are `reference_id` + `addresses[].first_name/last_name/street_1/zip_code/country_code` (ISO-2) + `line_items[]`, envelope `{"data":[…]}` for some routes, bare object for others. **G3**: `get_quote()` reads `price_quote/shipping_estimate/quote_expires_at` — none exist; real response uses proto-`Money` shape `{currency_code, units, nanos}` for `order_sub_total/order_shipping_fee/order_tax/order_discount/order_handle_fee/order_total`. **Confirmed working**: auth headers, base URL, HMAC algorithm round-trip + tamper/timestamp defenses (function-level), webhook controller mounted on staging. **Re-scope (4 phases)**: (1) live `/orders/draft` + `/orders/price` + `/orders/draft/labeled` discovery against sandbox until working bodies captured into `specs/004-fulfillment-routing/quickstart.md`; (2) regenerate `gearment_payload.py` dataclass + rewrite all 3 URLs in `gearment_adapter.py` (will break P0-18b1 mock tests as expected); (3) draft → quote → operator-review → confirm state machine + wizard; (4) topic parity (`tracking_updated` vs `tracking_order_updated`) via real Gearment dashboard simulator probe. **Surprises**: (S1) `.env` points at production not sandbox — Probe 2 may have created abandoned drafts; P4-01 PR must add sandbox URL switch + `.env.example` note. (S4) production throttles via 503 + Cloudflare timeouts not 429 — adapter needs `503/504` retry branch in addition to existing 429 path. ADR-001 split. |

## Decision log pointers

All architectural decisions live in `specs/006-master-plan/adrs/`:
- [ADR-001](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-001-spec-004-split.md) — Spec 004 split into 004a/004b/004c
- [ADR-002](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-002-drop-dual-sync-mode.md) — Drop dual sync mode
- [ADR-003](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-003-module-decomposition.md) — 4-module split
- [ADR-004](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-004-enterprise-alternatives.md) — Custom over Enterprise
- [ADR-005](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-005-carrier-unification.md) — Unified `shipping.carrier`
- [ADR-006](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-006-design-file-storage.md) — GDrive primary (revised 2026-04-13)
- [ADR-007](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-007-fulfillment-delegation-mixin.md) — Fulfillment delegation mixin
- [ADR-008](../../specs/006-master-plan/adrs/ADR-008-api-first-pivot.md) — API-first pivot (2026-04-13)

**Owner ad-hoc decisions (not yet ADR-lifted):**
- **2026-05-10 D1** — Bidirectional Etsy ingest parity (API ↔ email-parser produce identical `sale.order`/`sale.order.line`); `etsy_email_log_id` stays path-specific. → P0-22
- **2026-05-10 D2** — Many2one `label_status` with JPG seed; defer historical row mapping to a follow-up slice with owner-signed mapping table. → P1-LBL + P1-LBL-MIGRATE
- **2026-05-10 D3** — Operator-driven Gearment bulk-sync only; **no default label filter, no auto-trigger** ("we'll back on this later"). → P4-01 scope
- **2026-05-10 D4** — Etsy-tab tracking writeback = surface existing `sale.order.fulfillment` shipping fields read-only inside the Etsy tab; no new fields, no Etsy-API outbound push. → P4-01 scope
- **2026-05-10 D5** — `sale.order` form button is whole-order only; per-line/bulk-line lives on the dashboard. → P4-01 scope
- **2026-05-10 D6** — Slot the new work as new slices (P0-22, P1-01b, P1-LBL) + expand P4-01 scope. P1-01 stays `done`. → P1-01b is a refactor of the dashboard, not a re-open of P1-01.
- **2026-05-26 D7** — **Pause UAT (ESTY-182 user-guide story) + SKU Grammar v2 → v2.1 amendment**. UAT first run on `esty_odoo19` (Playwright `tests/e2e/uat_huong_dan_tao_san_pham.spec.ts` — commit `560fb58ac22`) surfaced feature-design gap: SKU Grammar v2 was preview-only advisory. Initial response (drafted as FEATURE_GAPS doc): add 4 slices to close gap (P-HUB-DESIGN-CODE-REGISTRY → P-HUB-SKU-BUILDER → P-HUB-V2-VALIDATE-ON-CREATE ∥ P-HUB-MISSING-INFO-WIZARD). Owner challenge later same day: "Do we really need DSGN in SKU?" Investigation revealed Excel catalog 0% DSGN usage + design is per-order not per-product. **v2.1 amendment**: removed DSGN segment + `sku.design.code` registry from grammar; consolidated A3 + FEATURE_GAPS into single canonical `docs/owner/SKU_GRAMMAR.md` with auto-Confluence sync via `.githooks/post-commit` pipeline. Net slice count: 3 (was 4); ~350 LOC total (was ~660 LOC). ESTY-183/184/185/186 stay JIRA `In Progress` with pause comment. End-user docs (HUONG_DAN_*/FLOW_*/BRD/SRS/STATUS) **deliberately NOT updated** in this pause — refreshed in one clean pass after features ship, without mentioning test-failure history. Engineering canonical: `docs/owner/SKU_GRAMMAR.md`. UAT resumes after `P-HUB-V2-VALIDATE-ON-CREATE` merge.

Vendor's second error (`has_front_back_or_whole_printing_option`) is a misleading double-report from the same invariant — our `printing_options` payload IS correct (verified via log id=151 `request_payload_summary`). Don't re-open the printing_options chase. | 006 (deployment scripts) + 004 (defensive UX) | P0-FIX-DEMO-NUMERIC-SKU + optional P4-01-FIX-NON-NUMERIC-SKU-WARNING | **closed** (demo-data half; P4-01-FIX-NON-NUMERIC-SKU-WARNING still proposed for defensive UX) | c09db19bd0f |

**Severity legend**: CRITICAL (data loss / pipeline crash) → stop run, hotfix immediately. HIGH (bug w/ workaround) → finish run, hotfix next session. MEDIUM (cosmetic) → bundle into next slice. LOW (polish) → defer to W7.

**State machine**: `surfaced` → `triaged` → `routed` → `fixing` → `verifying` → `closed`.

Four /learn-worthy surprises (now memory candidates): (i) PG savepoints CANNOT survive an outer-tx rollback — the plan's `savepoint(flush=False)`-survival premise was a misunderstanding of postgres semantics; durable audit must use `_logger.warning`, chatter is best-effort; test 3 rewritten with `assertLogs`. (ii) Odoo 19 `ir.actions.server` lives in `ir_act_server` (legacy short-name), not `ir_actions_server`. (iii) Cron records auto-create paired `ir.actions.server` ir_model_data row name-suffixed `_ir_actions_server` — both must be cleaned in any cron-removal migration. (iv) Migration scripts only fire when installed-version < manifest-version — bump 11→13 (skipping 12) was needed to force-trigger on environments that already took the prior manifest update without the script. Manifest: 19.0.1.0.11 → 19.0.1.0.13. **Migration runbook for staging**: in-flight `gearment_pod` orders without `x_gearment_outbound_ref` will try to push when their PO is re-confirmed — pre-stamp with sentinel ref OR confirm cleared before deploy.

- **2026-05-21**: **P1-XAPI-SHARED-SECRET-HEADER landed** after a deep-research detour into Etsy dev portal terminology (owner asked what "Keystring / Shared Secret / Personal Access / Rate Limit" actually mean). Sources: Etsy Open API v3 docs (Authentication, Rate Limits, Quickstart) + GitHub etsy/open-api Discussion #1521. Three substantive findings: (a) Keystring → OAuth2 `client_id`, Shared Secret → OAuth2 `client_secret` (Etsy renames the OAuth2-standard fields in their UI); (b) Etsy's own Quickstart example uses a 10-char shared_secret (`a1b2c3d4e5`) — retracts the P1-11-DEPLOY-STAGING "10 chars looks short" false alarm; (c) **Real defect surfaced**: Etsy began enforcing `x-api-key: keystring:secret` (combined form) on 2026-02-09 — our `EtsyApiClient._session()` still emitted keystring-only, so every API call after `active_source='api'` would 401. Fixed in a 5-line slice on `feature/006-master-plan-coding` (commit `4348cd32b79`): added `client_secret` non-empty validation at construction + combined-form header build + manifest 19.0.2.6.0→19.0.2.7.0 + 2 new tests + 1 mock backfill in `TestTokenHelpers.setUpClass`. Code+security review parallel: 0 CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM; 1 LOW deferred (mirror the fail-fast in `controllers/etsy_oauth._read_credentials`). Full suite 725 tests, 0 new failures. Staging deployed (`etsy_integration latest_version=19.0.2.7.0`). **Unblocks**: P1-11 pilot flip is now actually executable — without this slice the OAuth would succeed but every subsequent API call would 401.
- **2026-05-22**: **P1-11-WIRE-LIVE hotfix chain landed** — JaHandmadeArt pilot E2E on staging surfaced 5 interdependent OAuth/API defects between Authorize click and first receipt ingest. Chain in discovery order: (a) Etsy v3 token-exchange response has no `scope` field — controller's scope-validation contract was built on a wrong assumption; one-shot diagnostic captured the response keys (`access_token/api_key/expires_in/refresh_token/token_type/user_id`), reverted, then gated the validation on non-empty `scope` so the safety net stays armed if Etsy ever changes; (b) `etsy_shop._probe_api` had a constructor-signature bug + URL double-prefix bug + debug-level swallow (existing tests mocked `_probe_api` itself so the body was never exercised); (c) `services.etsy_api_client._read_credentials()` hardcoded path diverged from the OAuth controller's `etsy.oauth.credentials_path` system param — unified; (d) 403 handler discarded vendor body — body now in raised `ValueError` + warning log per memory `feedback_capture_response_body_before_blackbox_probe.md` precedent; (e) sandbox convention `etsy.shop.id == Etsy shop_id` (memory item #132) breaks on real shops (Etsy 403 `{"error":"User does not own Shop 10"}`) — added `etsy_api_shop_id` Char field, plumbed through 3 callers, backfilled JaHandmadeArt with `60752333`. E2E confirmed at 10:00:52 UTC: 1 `sale.order` ingested (`S00006`, Etsy receipt `3818231452`, ₫734,914.00, date 2025-10-06) + 1 listing + 5 variants. Hotfix mode — phases 1/2/4/8 skipped with owner approval; follow-up slice **P1-11-SHOPID-BOOTSTRAP** (`todo`) files deferred bootstrap-on-OAuth + tests + C-ESY-003 constraint + migration. Staging operational ahead of pilot owner sign-off. Side-tightening during the run: `list_db=False` + `dbfilter=^esty_odoo19$` (multi-DB OAuth state-binding risk) and `proxy_mode=True` (was `False` despite nginx HTTPS front). Manifest etsy_integration 19.0.2.7.0 → **19.0.2.8.0**. **Unblocks**: P1-11 (jahandmadeart pilot sourcing receipts via API live).
